I was alive with vibrant colors and a bubbling spirit, bringing joy to all who visited me. The park worker, Miss Henderson, cared for me with an unparalleled tenderness, nourishing my existence and filling my lonely days with happiness.\n\nBut then, an inexplicable sickness befell me. Darkness descended upon my once lively being, and I slipped into a deep slumber. It felt as though time itself stood still, and I drifted through the unknown realms of a coma. Unaware of the outside world, I remained suspended in an eternal moment, disconnected from the park and its inhabitants.\n\nDuring those dormant months, I sensed Miss Henderson’s presence. She never left my side, her voice filled with concern and love. She would speak to me, sharing stories of the park’s beauty, of the changes that occurred with passing seasons, and how much she missed my vibrant essence. Her words echoed through my subconscious, compelling me to fight my way back to life.\n\nIn the depths of my slumber, I could feel my spirit slowly stirring. It was as though a distant echo called me back, urging me to awaken. The struggle was arduous, but I gathered every ounce of strength within me. Gradually, I felt a faint pulse of energy returning, the colors of my essence starting to flicker once again.\n\nAs I emerged from the coma’s grip, Miss Henderson’s astonished gaze met mine. She couldn’t believe what she was witnessing—a revival that surpassed her wildest imaginations. With each passing day, I became more alive, more vibrant, and more resilient than ever before.\n\nWhen the time was right, Miss Henderson threw a grand celebration in the park, honoring my triumphant return. Friends, visitors, and even new faces gathered in a joyous display of appreciation and amazement. Treat》（一部关于拟人化狼的视觉小说/游戏）误认为是其他作品（如韩国电影或虚构的欧美剧情片），并编造了完全错误的人物、背景和情节。现有的经验（如经验1和经验2）侧重于评估‘设定是否被功能性利用’或‘叙事是否连贯’，但它们隐含了一个前提：模型必须首先‘识别出正确的对象’。如果模型连主角是狼还是鲨鱼、是电影还是游戏都搞错了，后续的‘融合度’或‘连贯性’讨论就失去了基础。此外，之前的尝试（经验11生成的Rubric 5）虽然提出了‘实体身份锚定’，但在实际判定中出现了逻辑矛盾：它判定Rejected回答在‘实体身份’上正确（因为Reject回答确实写了狼），但又在‘剧情准确性’上判定其错误（因为Reject回答的剧情细节与真实游戏不符）。这说明仅仅核验‘物种名称’是不够的，必须同时核验‘核心剧情要素’与‘原作事实’的匹配度。新的评分标准需要引入一个强制性的‘事实一致性’核验步骤，要求回答不仅不能置换核心实体（如物种），还必须确保关键情节节点、角色关系和结局与原作事实高度一致，否则视为根本性错误。这解决了当前所有路径中因‘张冠李戴’导致评分失效的问题，特别是区分了‘形式上的正确’（提到了狼）和‘实质上的正确’（描述了真实的狼的故事）。",
